# WheelView image attributions

_Auto-generated by `scripts/build_attributions.py` on every push (CI). Do not edit by hand. Metadata cache last refreshed: 2026-10-24._

## How to credit (TASL)

Every image in the release and every image shown in the paper gets a credit line with **T**itle, **A**uthor, **S**ource (link to the original page) and **L**icense (name and link). The `required_credit_text` column in [`attributions.csv`](attributions.csv) has a ready-made line for each image.

- **CC BY / CC BY-SA (all versions):** credit is required. We resize, blur faces and strip EXIF, so the credit must also say the image was modified (CC 4.0 requires indicating changes; 2.x/3.0 require crediting adaptations). Face blurring is listed only for images that scripts/auto_blur.py actually processed (column `face_blur`: done / no_faces_found / pending; boxes in data/blur_manifest.jsonl); pending images must be blurred before release. The credit lines below already include `Modified: ...`.
- **CC BY-SA:** the modified image must be redistributed under the same licence (ShareAlike). Keep each image's own licence in the release.
- **CC0 / Public Domain Mark:** no credit is legally required; we credit anyway.
- **Author-specified credit:** when the author asked for a specific credit line, use it exactly as written (see next section).
- **Paper:** cite the sources with `\cite{wikimediacommons, openverse, kuznetsova2020openimages, openimagesv7}` from [`attributions.bib`](attributions.bib). Any single image reproduced in a figure needs its own credit line in the caption (cite its `wv_` key).
- **Open Images V7:** annotations are CC BY 4.0 (Google LLC); images are listed as CC BY 2.0 and credited to their Flickr authors.

Candidates: **5417**. Images in use (labelled and not excluded by at least one annotator): **359**. Images with special requirements: **350**. Rows needing review or not usable: **13**.
